const YR=String(new Date().getFullYear()),EXP='Exposure date (animal bite)',D0='PEP day 0: date given',D3='PEP day 3: date given',D7='PEP day 7: date given',D4='PEP dose 4 (day 14-28): date given',VST='Vaccine dose status',ADM='Admission date',SON='Symptom onset date',DTH='Death date',LAT='Exposure latitude (decimal degrees)',LON='Exposure longitude (decimal degrees)',CATG='Rabies exposure category',PEPK='PEP course completed';

/* Filtru: Tinan · Munisípiu (in = Baucau / out = Fora husi Munisípiu) · Postu · SSK · Postu Saúde · Semana
   Kazu Fora husi Munisípiu: filtru Postu tuir postu husi SSK / Postu Saúde ne'ebé trata kazu */
const inPost=(r,p)=>ssksOf(U.mun,p).some(f=>nz(f)===nz(r[SSK]))||hpsOf(U.mun,p).some(s=>nz(s)===nz(r[HPK]));
/* Opsaun espesiál iha filtru Postu / SSK / Postu Saúde: Viqueque, Lospalos, Seluk */
/* Opsaun 'Fora husi Munisípiu' iha filtru Postu / SSK / Postu Saúde: lee husi sheet 'option' (kolun Postu administrativu)
   — naran hotu ne'ebé la'ós postu Baucau (ex. Viqueque, Lautem, Dili) + 'Seluk' iha ikus */
const OUTDEF=['Viqueque','Lautem','Dili'],RXLP=/lospalos|laut[eé]m/i;
const kz=v=>nz(v).normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'');
const isBP=v=>{const a=kz(v);return !!a&&postsOf(U.mun).some(p=>{const b=kz(p);if(a===b)return true;let i=0;while(i<a.length&&i<b.length&&a[i]===b[i])i++;return i>=5})};
/* Postu 'Fora husi Munisípiu': husi sheet 'informasaun jeografia' (liña ho Munisípiu la'ós munisípiu uzuáriu), depois lista opsaun */
function outPosts(){const L=[],add=v=>{v=String(v||'').trim();if(!v||/^seluk$/i.test(v)||isBP(v)||L.some(x=>nz(x)===nz(v)))return;L.push(v)};
GEO.filter(g=>nz(g.m)!==nz(U.mun)).forEach(g=>add(g.p));const f=((META&&META.fields)||[]).find(x=>x.name===POSTN);(f&&f.options||[]).forEach(add);if(!L.length)OUTDEF.forEach(add);return L}
const outNames=()=>outPosts().concat('Seluk');
/* Lista SSK / Postu Saúde 'Fora husi Munisípiu' (k='f' SSK, k='s' Postu Saúde), tuir postu ne'ebé hili */
function outGeo(k,p,f){const L=[...new Set(GEO.filter(g=>nz(g.m)!==nz(U.mun)&&(!p||nz(g.p)===nz(p))&&(k==='f'||!f||nz(g.f)===nz(f))).map(g=>String(g[k]||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))];return L.length?L:(p?[p]:outNames())}
const munOut=()=>{const f=((META&&META.fields)||[]).find(x=>x.name===MUN);return (f&&f.options||[]).concat(GEO.map(g=>g.m)).find(x=>x&&nz(x)!==nz(U.mun))||'Fora husi Munisípiu'};
/* Opsaun filtru 'Fora husi Munisípiu' ho kódigu espesiál (__out:Naran / __SL = Seluk) */
const spKey=n=>/^seluk$/i.test(String(n).trim())?'__SL':'__out:'+n;
const outOpts=(L,x)=>L.map(spKey).filter((k,i,a)=>a.indexOf(k)===i).map(k=>`<option value="${esc(k)}" ${x===k?'selected':''}>${esc(spLbl(k))}</option>`).join('');
const isSp=v=>v==='__SL'||String(v||'').indexOf('__out:')===0,spLbl=v=>v==='__SL'?'Seluk':isSp(v)?String(v).slice(6):v;
const spOpts=x=>`<optgroup label="Fora husi Munisípiu">${outPosts().map(n=>'__out:'+n).concat('__SL').map(k=>`<option value="${esc(k)}" ${x===k?'selected':''}>${esc(spLbl(k))}</option>`).join('')}</optgroup>`;
const outRx=n=>RXLP.test(n)?RXLP:new RegExp('(^|[^a-z])'+nz(n).replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&')+'([^a-z]|$)','i');
function spMatch(r,k,val,list){const out=r.munCat==='out',txt=nz([val,out?r[MUN]:'',out?r[POSTN]:''].join(' '));
if(k!=='__SL')return outRx(spLbl(k)).test(txt);

const pMatch=r=>!FIL.post||(isFP()&&!!U.ssk)||(isSp(FIL.post)?spMatch(r,FIL.post,r[POSTN],postsOf(U.mun)):(r.munCat==='out'?inPost(r,FIL.post):nz(r[POSTN])===nz(FIL.post)));
const sMatch=r=>!FIL.ssk||(isSp(FIL.ssk)?spMatch(r,FIL.ssk,r[SSK],ssksOf(U.mun)):nz(r[SSK])===nz(FIL.ssk));
const hMatch=r=>!FIL.hp||(isSp(FIL.hp)?spMatch(r,FIL.hp,r[HPK],hpsOf(U.mun)):nz(r[HPK])===nz(FIL.hp));
const filt=()=>DATA.filter(r=>(!FIL.year||String(r['Reporting year'])===FIL.year)&&(isFP()?true:(!FIL.mun||r.munCat===FIL.mun))&&pMatch(r)&&sMatch(r)&&hMatch(r)&&(!FIL.week||parseInt(r['Reporting week'])===parseInt(FIL.week)));
/* Nivel kategoria espozisaun (1/2/3) husi valór hotu: Grade 3, Grau 3, Kategoria III, Category III, III, 3 */
/* ===== KPI: PEP kompletu (husi data dóze PEP) no Simu RIG (husi Detalle RIG) ===== */
/* Dóze PEP: 1 (loron 0), 2 (loron 3), 3 (loron 7), 4 (loron 14-28) */
/* Kazu elejível ba PEP: Planu rejime PEP iha valór (IM / ID / Sim), la'ós 'Lae' / 'Nao' */
const rigY=r=>/^(yes|sim)$/i.test(String(r['Rabies immunoglobulin received']||'').trim());
const planY=r=>{const v=String(r['Planned PEP regimen']||'').trim();return !!v&&!/^(no|lae|nao|não)$/i.test(v)};
const dTop=r=>r[D4]?4:r[D7]?3:r[D3]?2:r[D0]?1:0,dMax=dTop;
const pepDone=r=>{const g=String(r['PEP regimen used']||r['Planned PEP regimen']||''),m=dMax(r);return /booster/i.test(g)?m>=2:/ipc|\bid\b|intrad/i.test(g)?m>=3:m>=4};
const rigOk=r=>rigY(r)||!!(r['RIG date received']||r['RIG dose (IU)']||r['RIG volume (ml)']||r['Immunoglobulin name/code (brand)']);
const catLv=v=>{const t=String(v||'').trim().toUpperCase().replace(/^(GRADE|GRAU|KATEGORI[AU]?|CATEGOR(Y|IA)|CAT\.?)\s*/,'');return t==='III'||t==='3'?3:t==='II'||t==='2'?2:t==='I'||t==='1'?1:0};

function bars(t,e,o={}){if(!o.keep)e=e.sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);if(o.top)e=e.slice(0,o.top);const mx=Math.max(1,...e.map(x=>x[1]));

function pie(t,e){let SM=0;e=e.sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);const tot=e.reduce((s,x)=>s+x[1],0);if(!tot)return `<div class="card"><h4>${t}</h4>${none}</div>`;
let a=-Math.PI/2,s='';const P=(r,x)=>[(100+r*Math.cos(x)).toFixed(1),(100+r*Math.sin(x)).toFixed(1)];
e.forEach(([k,v],i)=>{const f=v/tot,b=a+f*2*Math.PI,c=PAL[i%8];
if(f>.9999)s+=`<circle cx="100" cy="100" r="66" fill="none" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="38"><title>${esc(tt(k))}: ${v}</title></circle>`;
else{const[x0,y0]=P(85,a),[x1,y1]=P(85,b),[x2,y2]=P(47,b),[x3,y3]=P(47,a),l=f>.5?1:0;s+=`<path d="M${x0} ${y0}A85 85 0 ${l} 1 ${x1} ${y1}L${x2} ${y2}A47 47 0 ${l} 0 ${x3} ${y3}Z" fill="${c}" style="stroke:var(--card)" stroke-width="2"><title>${esc(tt(k))}: ${v}</title></path>`}
if(f>=.06){const[tx,ty]=P(66,(a+b)/2);s+=`<text x="${tx}" y="${ty}" fill="#fff" font-size="12" font-weight="700" text-anchor="middle" dominant-baseline="middle">${v}</text>`}else if(v>0){SM++;const m=(a+b)/2,R2=SM%2?102:116,[lx,ly]=P(R2,m),[ex,ey]=P(87,m);s+=`<line x1="${ex}" y1="${ey}" x2="${lx}" y2="${ly}" style="stroke:var(--mut)" stroke-width="1"/><text x="${lx}" y="${ly}" font-size="11" font-weight="800" text-anchor="${Math.cos(m)>=0?'start':'end'}" dominant-baseline="middle" style="fill:var(--tx);paint-order:stroke;stroke:var(--card);stroke-width:3px">${v}</text>`}a=b});
return `<div class="card"><h4>${t}</h4><div class="pc"><svg width="210" height="210" viewBox="-28 -28 256 256" style="overflow:visible">${s}<text x="100" y="97" text-anchor="middle" font-size="24" font-weight="800" style="fill:var(--tx)">${tot}</text><text x="100" y="116" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" style="fill:var(--mut)">total</text></svg>
<div>${e.map(([k,v],i)=>`<div class="lg"><i style="background:${PAL[i%8]}"></i>${esc(tt(k))} <b>${v}</b> <small>${Math.round(v/tot*100)}%</small></div>`).join('')}</div></div></div>`}
function colc(t,e,o={}){if(!o.keep)e=e.sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);const mx=Math.max(1,...e.map(x=>x[1])),den=o.den||o.tot||e.reduce((s,x)=>s+x[1],0)||1;
return `<div class="card"><div class="ch"><h4>${t}</h4>${o.head||''}</div>${e.length?`<div class="cols ${o.cls||'w'}" style="height:220px">${e.map(([k,v],i)=>`<div class="col" title="${esc(tt(k))}: ${v}"><em>${o.hide0&&!v?'&nbsp;':v}${o.pct?`<br><small>${Math.round(v/den*100)}%</small>`:''}</em><i style="height:${v/mx*70}%${o.hl&&v===mx&&v?';background:linear-gradient(0deg,#c2412d,#fb8a6e)!important':''}"></i><span>${esc(tt(k))}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>`:none}</div>`}
let TR='exp';

function trend(R){const m={};let skip=0;R.forEach(r=>{let y,w;if(TR==='exp'){if(!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(r[EXP])){skip++;return}[y,w]=wy(r[EXP])}else{y=parseInt(r['Reporting year']);w=parseInt(r['Reporting week']);if(isNaN(y)||isNaN(w)){skip++;return}}m[y*100+w]=(m[y*100+w]||0)+1});

function fbar(){if(isFP())FIL.mun='in';const lockS=isFP()&&!!U.ssk;if(lockS&&FIL.post)FIL.post='';if(lockS&&FIL.ssk&&!isSp(FIL.ssk))FIL.ssk='';const yrs=[...new Set(DATA.map(r=>String(r['Reporting year'])).filter(Boolean).concat(YR))].sort().reverse(),M=FIL.mun,PU=lockPost(),lock=PU&&M!=='out',o=(l,x)=>l.map(i=>`<option ${x===i?'selected':''}>${esc(i)}</option>`).join('');
/* Munisípiu = Baucau → de'it postu / SSK / Postu Saúde Baucau; Fora husi Munisípiu → de'it Viqueque, Lautem, Dili, Seluk; Munisípiu hotu → rua hotu */

Liña verde aas = di\'ak.'})+pontTbl(R)+`<div class="card"><h4>Rezumu intervalu (loron)</h4><div class="tw"><table><tr><th>Intervalu</th><th>n</th><th>Mediana</th><th>Variasaun</th></tr>${iv.map(([l,fn])=>{const a=R.map(fn).filter(x=>x!==null);return `<tr><td>${l}</td><td>${a.length}</td><td>${a.length?med(a):'–'}</td><td>${a.length?Math.min(...a)+' – '+Math.max(...a):'–'}</td></tr>`}).join('')}</table></div></div>`+(cs.length?`<div class="card"><h4>Kazu ho sintoma / baixa / mate</h4><div class="tw"><table><tr><th>Rejistu</th><th>Tata</th><th>PEP dóze 1</th><th>Sintoma hahú</th><th>Baixa</th><th>Mate</th></tr>${cs.map(r=>`<tr><td>${esc(r['Record No.'])}</td><td>${f(r[EXP]||null)}</td><td>${f(r[D0]||null)}</td><td>${f(r[SON]||null)}</td><td>${f(r[ADM]||null)}</td><td>${f(r[DTH]||null)}</td></tr>`).join('')}</table></div></div>`:'')}
if(v==='map'){let bad=0;R.forEach(r=>{const la=parseFloat(String(r[LAT]).replace(',','.')),lo=parseFloat(String(r[LON]).replace(',','.'));if(isNaN(la)||isNaN(lo))return;if(la<-9.6||la>-8||lo<124||lo>127.4){bad++;return}pts.push([la,lo,r])});h+=`<div class="card"><div class="ch"><h4>Fatin espozisaun <small>kazu ${pts.length} husi ${N} iha mapa${bad?` · ${bad} iha li'ur Timor-Leste`:''}</small></h4><span class="mlg">${Object.entries(MCOL).map(([k,c])=>`<span><i style="background:${c}"></i>${tt(k)}</span>`).join('')}</span></div><div id="map" style="height:480px;border-radius:12px;overflow:hidden"></div></div>`}
$('#view').innerHTML=h;if(isFP())fpTidy();if(v==='map')initMap(pts)}
/* ===== Focal Point: hatudu de'it grafiku ho dadus husi nia SSK =====
   - hamoos kartaun 'Laiha dadus' (grafiku ne'ebé la iha relasaun ho dadus SSK ne'e)
   - subar filtru xave (Munisípiu / Postu / SSK) no hatudu naran SSK iha leten */
function fpTidy(){const V=$('#view');
V.querySelectorAll('.card').forEach(c=>{const kids=[...c.children];if(kids.some(k=>k.tagName==='P'&&k.classList.contains('mut')&&k.textContent.trim()==='Laiha dadus'))c.remove()});
V.querySelectorAll('.grid').forEach(g=>{if(!g.children.length)g.remove()});
const fb=V.querySelector('.fb');if(fb){fb.querySelectorAll('label').forEach(l=>{const s=l.querySelector('select');if(s&&s.disabled)l.style.display='none'});
 const t=document.createElement('label');t.className='fpssk';t.innerHTML=`<span class="fi">🏥</span><span class="ft"><small>Dadus husi</small><b>${esc(U.ssk||U.post)}</b></span>`;fb.prepend(t)}
if(!V.querySelector('.card,.kpi')){const p=document.createElement('div');p.className='card';p.innerHTML='<p class="mut" style="text-align:center;margin:30px 0">Seidauk iha dadus husi '+esc(U.ssk||U.post)+' ba filtru ne\'e.</p>';V.append(p)}}
/* ===== Pontualidade PEP dóze 1: tabela kada fulan, indikadór xave no konkluzaun ===== */
function pontTbl(R){const M=mRange(R),tot=R.length;if(!M)return '';const by={};let bad=0,mx=0;
R.forEach(r=>{const g=gap(r[EXP],r[D0]);if(g===null){bad++;return}mx=Math.max(mx,g);const k=mKey(r);if(!k)return;const o=by[k]=by[k]||{a:0,b:0};g<=1?o.a++:o.b++});
const rows=M.K.map((k,i)=>{const o=by[k]||{a:0,b:0},n=o.a+o.b;return {l:M.L[i],a:o.a,b:o.b,n,p:n?o.a/n:null}}),T=rows.reduce((s,x)=>({a:s.a+x.a,b:s.b+x.b}),{a:0,b:0});T.n=T.a+T.b;T.p=T.n?T.a/T.n:null;
const st=p=>p===null?'<span class="mut">–</span>':p<.5?'<span class="bd n">Kritiku</span>':p<.7?'<span class="bd w">Presiza atensaun</span>':'<span class="bd y">Di\'ak</span>';
const pc=p=>p===null?'–':Math.round(p*100)+'%',bg=p=>p===null?'':p<.5?'background:color-mix(in srgb,#e8674f 22%,transparent)':p>=.8?'background:color-mix(in srgb,#2e9e48 18%,transparent)':'';
const V=rows.filter(x=>x.n),best=V.length?V.reduce((m,x)=>x.p>m.p?x:m):null,worst=V.length?V.reduce((m,x)=>x.p<m.p?x:m):null,busy=V.length?V.reduce((m,x)=>x.n>m.n?x:m):null;
const tbl=`<div class="card w2"><h4>Pontualidade PEP dóze 1 kada fulan — tabela</h4><div class="tw"><table><tr><th>Fulan</th><th>Tempu loos (≤1 loron)</th><th>Tardi (&gt;1 loron)</th><th>Total kazu</th><th>% tempu loos</th><th>% tardi</th><th>Estatutu</th></tr>`+
rows.map(x=>`<tr><td><b>${esc(x.l)}</b></td><td>${x.a}</td><td>${x.b}</td><td>${x.n}</td><td style="${bg(x.p)};font-weight:700">${pc(x.p)}</td><td>${x.p===null?'–':pc(1-x.p)}</td><td>${st(x.p)}</td></tr>`).join('')+
`<tr style="font-weight:800"><td>Total</td><td>${T.a}</td><td>${T.b}</td><td>${T.n}</td><td style="${bg(T.p)}">${pc(T.p)}</td><td>${T.p===null?'–':pc(1-T.p)}</td><td>${st(T.p)}</td></tr></table></div>
<p class="mut" style="font-size:11.5px;margin:8px 0 0;text-align:center">Tempu loos = PEP dóze 1 iha loron hanesan ka loron 1 hafoin tata (WHO: PEP lalais liu). Fulan tuir Data rejistu.</p></div>`;
const K=`<div class="kpis">${kpi('Kazu konta iha pontualidade',T.n,'husi '+tot+' kazu')}${kpi('Kazu la konta',bad,'data mamuk / la válidu')}${kpi('% tempu loos (total)',pc(T.p),T.a+' husi '+T.n)}${kpi("Fulan di'ak liu",best?`<span class="kpiw">${esc(best.l)}</span>`:'–',best?pc(best.p)+' tempu loos':'')}${kpi('Fulan aat liu',worst?`<span class="kpiw">${esc(worst.l)}</span>`:'–',worst?pc(worst.p)+' tempu loos':'')}${kpi('Fulan ho kazu barak liu',busy?`<span class="kpiw">${esc(busy.l)}</span>`:'–',busy?busy.n+' kazu':'')}</div>`;
const C=[];if(T.p!==null)C.push(`Kuaze ${Math.round(T.p*10)} husi 10 pasiente simu PEP dóze 1 iha tempu loos (≤1 loron hafoin tata).`);
rows.filter(x=>x.p!==null&&x.p<.5).forEach(x=>C.push(`<b>${esc(x.l)}</b>: kritiku — pasiente tardi (${x.b}) barak liu tempu loos (${x.a}). Verifika stock vasina, asesu ka atrazu rejistu.`));
if(busy&&busy.b>0)C.push(`<b>${esc(busy.l)}</b>: kazu barak liu (${busy.n}); tardi ${busy.b} (${pc(1-busy.p)}).`);
if(bad)C.push(`Kazu ${bad} la konta tanba data tata ka data dóze 1 mamuk / la válidu — kompleta iha Jestaun Dadus.`);
if(mx>30)C.push(`Intervalu máximu ${mx} loron — posivelmente erru hakerek tinan. Verifika data dóze 1 / data tata.`);
const cc=`<div class="card"><h4>Konkluzaun no rekomendasaun</h4><ul style="margin:6px 0 0;padding-left:18px;line-height:1.7;font-size:13px">${C.map(x=>`<li>${x}</li>`).join('')}</ul></div>`;
return K+`<div class="grid">${tbl}${cc}</div>`}
/* ===== Mapa ba PDF: kria imajen rasik husi mapa (pozisaun titik kazu exatu, la muda) =====
   Tile mapa: pozisaun loos husi ekran. Titik kazu: kalkula husi latitude/longitude (MAP.latLngToContainerPoint). */
function mapSnap(){try{if(!MAP||typeof L==='undefined')return null;const el=$('#map'),W=el.clientWidth,H=el.clientHeight;if(!W||!H)return null;
const k=2,dark=document.documentElement.dataset.theme==='dark',er=el.getBoundingClientRect();
const draw=withTiles=>{const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=W*k;c.height=H*k;const x=c.getContext('2d');x.scale(k,k);
 x.fillStyle=dark?'#1b2a22':'#eef3ee';x.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
 if(withTiles){if(dark)x.filter='invert(1) hue-rotate(180deg) brightness(.85) contrast(.9)';
  el.querySelectorAll('img.leaflet-tile').forEach(t=>{if(!t.complete||!t.naturalWidth)return;const r=t.getBoundingClientRect();x.drawImage(t,r.left-er.left,r.top-er.top,r.width,r.height)});x.filter='none'}
 MAPPTS.forEach(([la,lo,r])=>{const p=MAP.latLngToContainerPoint([la,lo]);if(p.x<-10||p.y<-10||p.x>W+10||p.y>H+10)return;
  x.beginPath();x.arc(p.x,p.y,7,0,Math.PI*2);x.fillStyle=MCOL['Grade '+catLv(r[CATG])]||'#94a3b8';x.globalAlpha=.9;x.fill();x.globalAlpha=1;x.lineWidth=1.5;x.strokeStyle='#fff';x.stroke()});
 x.font='11px sans-serif';x.fillStyle=dark?'#ddd':'#333';x.textAlign='right';x.fillText('© OpenStreetMap',W-6,H-6);
 return c.toDataURL('image/png')};
try{return draw(true)}catch(e){return draw(false)} /* se tile la permite (CORS), titik kazu sempre loos */
}catch(e){console.log(e);return null}}
/* ===== Rezumu jerál: grafik foun ===== */
/* Ranking Top 10: númeru 1-10, naran, kazu no % (husi kazu hotu) */
function rank(t,e,tot,blank){e=e.slice().sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);const top=e.slice(0,10),mx=Math.max(1,...top.map(x=>x[1])),sum=top.reduce((s,x)=>s+x[1],0),pc=v=>tot?Math.round(v/tot*100)+'%':'–';
return `<div class="card"><h4>${esc(t)}</h4>${top.length?top.map(([k,v],i)=>`<div class="rk t${i+1}" title="${esc(k)}: ${v} kazu (${pc(v)})"><span class="n">${i+1}</span><div class="bx"><i style="width:${Math.max(3,v/mx*100)}%"></i><span><em>${esc(tt(k))}</em><b>${v}<small>${pc(v)}</small></b></span></div></div>`).join(''):none}</div>`}
/* Sexu: kaixa Mane / Feto ho ikon, númeru no % */
function sexC(R){const m=R.filter(r=>r.Sex==='Male').length,f=R.filter(r=>r.Sex==='Female').length,t=m+f,o=R.length-t,pc=v=>t?Math.round(v/t*100):0;
if(!t)return `<div class="card"><h4>Sexu</h4>${none}</div>`;
return `<div class="card"><h4>Sexu</h4><div class="sx"><div class="sxt m"><span class="sxi">♂</span><b>${m}</b><small>Mane · ${pc(m)}%</small></div><div class="sxt f"><span class="sxi">♀</span><b>${f}</b><small>Feto · ${pc(f)}%</small></div></div><div class="sxbar"><i class="m" style="width:${pc(m)}%"></i><i class="f" style="width:${100-pc(m)}%"></i></div></div>`}
/* ===== Grafiku ombak: liña suave ho área gradiente, pontu no valór ===== */
const WPAL=['#14b8a6','#fb8a6e','#0e5f73','#f59e0b'];let WID=0;
const niceMax=v=>{if(v<=8)return Math.max(4,Math.ceil(v/4)*4);const p=Math.pow(10,Math.floor(Math.log10(v))),m=v/p,st=[1,1.2,1.6,2,2.4,3,4,5,6,8,10];return st.find(x=>x>=m)*p};
function smooth(P,t,b){if(P.length<2)return `M${P[0][0]},${P[0][1]}`;const c=v=>Math.min(b,Math.max(t-8,v));let d=`M${P[0][0].toFixed(1)},${P[0][1].toFixed(1)}`;
for(let i=0;i<P.length-1;i++){const p0=P[i-1]||P[i],p1=P[i],p2=P[i+1],p3=P[i+2]||p2;d+=` C${(p1[0]+(p2[0]-p0[0])/6).toFixed(1)},${c(p1[1]+(p2[1]-p0[1])/6).toFixed(1)} ${(p2[0]-(p3[0]-p1[0])/6).toFixed(1)},${c(p2[1]-(p3[1]-p1[1])/6).toFixed(1)} ${p2[0].toFixed(1)},${p2[1].toFixed(1)}`}return d}
function wave(t,L,S,o={}){const n=L.length,cls=o.cls||'',hd=`<div class="ch"><h4>${t}</h4>${o.head||''}</div>`,ft='';
if(!n||!S.some(s=>s.v.some(v=>v>0)))return `<div class="card ${cls}">${hd}${none}${ft}</div>`;
const W=o.W||640,H=o.H||(o.rot?270:240),pl=38,pr=18,pt=26,pb=o.rot?52:30,iw=W-pl-pr,ih=H-pt-pb,base=pt+ih,top=niceMax(Math.max(1,...S.flatMap(s=>s.v)));
const X=i=>pl+(n===1?iw/2:i*iw/(n-1)),Y=v=>base-v/top*ih;let g='';
for(let k=0;k<=4;k++){const v=top*k/4,y=Y(v).toFixed(1);g+=`<line x1="${pl}" x2="${W-pr}" y1="${y}" y2="${y}" class="wg"/><text x="${pl-6}" y="${(+y+3.5).toFixed(1)}" class="wy">${Math.round(v)}</text>`}
const step=Math.ceil(n/(o.maxLab||14));let xl='';L.forEach((l,i)=>{if(i%step&&i!==n-1)return;xl+=o.rot?`<text transform="translate(${X(i).toFixed(1)},${base+10}) rotate(-60)" class="wxr">${esc(l)}</text>`:`<text x="${X(i).toFixed(1)}" y="${base+19}" class="wx">${esc(l)}</text>`});
let body='',pts='';S.forEach((s,si)=>{const c=s.c||WPAL[si%4],id='wgr'+(++WID),P=s.v.map((v,i)=>[X(i),Y(v)]),d=smooth(P,pt,base),mxv=Math.max(...s.v),many=n>16;
body+=`<defs><linearGradient id="${id}" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="${c}" stop-opacity="${S.length>1?.16:.34}"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="${c}" stop-opacity="0"/></linearGradient></defs><path d="${d} L${X(n-1).toFixed(1)},${base} L${X(0).toFixed(1)},${base} Z" fill="url(#${id})"/><path d="${d}" fill="none" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" class="wl"/>`;
s.v.forEach((v,i)=>{pts+=`<circle cx="${P[i][0].toFixed(1)}" cy="${P[i][1].toFixed(1)}" r="${many?2.2:3.3}" fill="var(--card)" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.6"><title>${esc(s.name||'')} · ${esc(L[i])}: ${v}</title></circle>`})});
/* Númeru iha pontu hotu: valór aas iha leten, seluk iha okos; la sobrepoin malu */
const many=n>16;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const it=S.map((s,si)=>({v:s.v[i],y:Y(s.v[i]),c:s.c||WPAL[si%4]})).sort((a,b)=>a.y-b.y);const ly=[];it.forEach((o2,k)=>{let y=k===0?o2.y-10:o2.y+19;if(k&&y<ly[k-1]+13)y=ly[k-1]+13;ly.push(y)});for(let k=ly.length-1;k>=0;k--)ly[k]=Math.min(ly[k],k===ly.length-1?base-4:ly[k+1]-13);
it.forEach((o2,k)=>{pts+=`<text x="${X(i).toFixed(1)}" y="${ly[k].toFixed(1)}" class="wv" fill="${o2.c}"${many?' style="font-size:9.5px"':''}>${o2.v}</text>`})}
const leg=S.length>1?`<div class="wlg">${S.map((s,si)=>`<span><i style="background:${s.c||WPAL[si%4]}"></i>${esc(s.name)}${s.tot!=null?` <b>${s.tot}</b>`:''}</span>`).join('')}</div>`:'';
return `<div class="card ${cls}">${hd}<svg viewBox="0 0 ${W} ${H}" width="${W}" height="${H}" class="wv-svg" role="img" aria-label="${esc(String(t).replace(/<[^>]*>/g,''))}">${g}${body}${pts}${xl}</svg>${leg}${ft}</div>`}
const waveE=(t,e,o)=>wave(t,e.map(x=>x[0]),[{name:'Kazu',v:e.map(x=>x[1])}],{head:o.head,foot:o.foot,rot:1,maxLab:60,W:1150,H:300});
/* Kazu kada fulan (tuir Data rejistu) ba seri ida ka barak */
const mKey=r=>{const d=String(r[RD]||'');return /^\d{4}-\d{2}/.test(d)?+d.slice(0,4)*100+ +d.slice(5,7):0};
function mRange(R){const ks=[...new Set(R.map(mKey).filter(Boolean))];if(!ks.length)return null;let k=Math.min(...ks);const end=Math.max(...ks),yr=Math.floor(k/100)!==Math.floor(end/100),K=[];
while(k<=end){K.push(k);k=k%100===12?(Math.floor(k/100)+1)*100+1:k+1}return {K,L:K.map(k=>MESES[k%100-1]+(yr?' '+String(Math.floor(k/100)).slice(2):''))}}
function mWave(t,R,defs,o={}){const M=mRange(R);if(!M)return wave(t,[],[],o);const by={};R.forEach(r=>{const k=mKey(r);if(k)(by[k]=by[k]||[]).push(r)});
return wave(t,M.L,defs.map(d=>({name:d.name,c:d.c,v:M.K.map(k=>(by[k]||[]).filter(d.f).length),tot:R.filter(d.f).length})),o)}
const MESES=['Jan','Fev','Mar','Abr','Mai','Jun','Jul','Ago','Set','Out','Nov','Dez'];
/* Kazu kada fulan tuir Data rejistu (fulan mamuk mós hatudu ho 0) */
function monthC(R){const sk=R.filter(r=>!mKey(r)).length;return mWave('Kazu kada fulan (tuir data rejistu)',R,[{name:'Kazu',f:()=>true}],{cls:'w2',foot:sk?`Kazu ${sk} laiha data rejistu válidu.`:''})}
/* Estatutu dóze PEP — hanesan pájina PEP no RIG (kada kazu konta dala ida tuir dóze ikus) */
function pepC(R){const el=R.filter(r=>planY(r)||dTop(r)>0),M=el.length,lv=i=>el.filter(r=>dTop(r)===i).length;
if(!M)return `<div class="card"><h4>Estatutu dóze PEP</h4>${none}</div>`;
return colc(`Estatutu dóze PEP (kazu ${M} elejível)`,[["Dóze 1 de'it",lv(1)],["To'o dóze 2",lv(2)],["To'o dóze 3",lv(3)],['Kompletu 4 dóze',lv(4)]].concat(lv(0)?[['Seidauk simu',lv(0)]]:[]),{keep:1,pct:1,den:M,foot:`Kompletu 4 dóze: ${lv(4)} husi ${M} (${Math.round(lv(4)/M*100)}%).`})}
/* Kategoria III simu RIG */
function rig3C(R){const g=R.filter(r=>catLv(r[CATG])===3),y=g.filter(r=>rigY(r)).length;
return pie(`Kategoria III simu RIG (kazu ${g.length})`,g.length?[['Simu RIG',y],['La simu RIG',g.length-y]]:[])}
const MCOL={'Grade 1':'#FFC726','Grade 2':'#f08a00','Grade 3':'#DC241F'};
let MAPPTS=[];function initMap(pts){MAPPTS=pts;if(typeof L==='undefined'){$('#map').innerHTML='<p class="mut" style="padding:20px">Labele karrega bibliotéka mapa.</p>';return}MAP&&MAP.remove();MAP=L.map('map').setView([-8.47,126.45],9);L.tileLayer('https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png',{attribution:'© OpenStreetMap',crossOrigin:'anonymous'}).addTo(MAP);pts.forEach(([la,lo,r])=>L.circleMarker([la,lo],{radius:7,color:'#fff',weight:1.5,fillColor:MCOL['Grade '+catLv(r[CATG])]||'#94a3b8',fillOpacity:.9}).bindPopup(`<b>Rejistu #${esc(r['Record No.'])}</b><br>${esc(r[EXP]||'')} · ${esc(tt(r['Type of exposure']))}<br>${esc(tt(r[CATG]||''))} · ${esc(tt(r['Animal species']))}<br>${esc(r[POSTN])}`).addTo(MAP));if(pts.length)MAP.fitBounds(pts.map(x=>[x[0],x[1]]),{maxZoom:13,padding:[20,20]})}
const kpi=(l,v,s)=>`<div class="kpi"><span>${l}</span><b>${v}</b><span>${s||''}</span></div>`;
const calcAge=(d,r)=>{const a=new Date(d),b=new Date(r);let y=b.getFullYear()-a.getFullYear();if(b.getMonth()<a.getMonth()||(b.getMonth()===a.getMonth()&&b.getDate()<a.getDate()))y--;return y<0?'':y};

function formLoc(lv){const q=n=>document.querySelector(`#view [data-name="${n}"]`),m=q(MUN),ps=q(POSTN),ss=q(SSK),hp=q(HPK);if(!ps)return;

if(lv<=0){fill(ps,out?outNames():(PL?[U.post]:postsOf(U.mun)),init,'—');if(PU&&U.ssk&&!out&&!ps.value&&postsOf(U.mun).includes(U.post))ps.value=U.post}
const p=ps.value,pb=!out&&postsOf(U.mun).includes(p)?p:'';
const LK=PU&&!!U.ssk;
if(lv<=1){fill(ss,LK?[U.ssk]:PU?ssksOf(U.mun,U.post):out?outGeo('f',p):ssksOf(U.mun,pb),init,out||pb?'—':'Hili postu uluk');if(ss){const lk=LK;if(lk)ss.value=U.ssk;ss.classList.toggle('autof',lk);ss.tabIndex=lk?-1:0;ss.title=lk?'Fasilidade ita-nia konta (xave)':''}}
if(lv<=2)fill(hp,LK?hpsOf(U.mun,'',U.ssk):PU?hpsOf(U.mun,U.post,ss?ss.value:''):out?outGeo('s',p,ss?ss.value:''):hpsOf(U.mun,pb,ss?ss.value:''),init,'—');
CURPOST=p}

function grpOpt(e,a){if(a===''||isNaN(a))return '';if(e&&e.tagName==='SELECT'){for(const o of e.options){const t=o.text+' '+o.value,n=(t.match(/\d+/g)||[]).map(Number);if(!o.value)continue;

const MONS={jan:1,fev:2,feb:2,mar:3,abr:4,apr:4,mai:5,may:5,jun:6,jul:7,ago:8,agu:8,aug:8,set:9,sep:9,out:10,oct:10,nov:11,dez:12,dec:12};
/* Data → yyyy-mm-dd. Formatu: yyyy-mm-dd, dd/mm/yyyy, dd-Fulan-yyyy (ex. 24-Abr-1996). Data la válidu (31-Jun, ?-Jul) → '' */
function parseD(v){v=String(v||'').trim();if(!v)return '';const ok=(y,mo,d)=>{const t=new Date(+y,+mo-1,+d);return t.getFullYear()==+y&&t.getMonth()==+mo-1&&t.getDate()==+d?y+'-'+String(mo).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+String(d).padStart(2,'0'):''};
let m=v.match(/^(\d{4})[-\/.](\d{1,2})[-\/.](\d{1,2})(?:$|[T\s])/);if(m)return ok(m[1],m[2],m[3]);
m=v.match(/^(\d{1,2})[-\/.](\d{1,2})[-\/.](\d{4})$/);if(m)return ok(m[3],m[2],m[1]);
m=v.match(/^(\d{1,2})[\s\-\/.]+([A-Za-zçÇ]{3,})\.?[\s\-\/.]+(\d{4})$/);if(m){const mo=MONS[m[2].toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g,'').slice(0,3)];return mo?ok(m[3],mo,m[1]):''}
return ''}

function mtable(){const lock=lockPost(),o=(l,x)=>l.map(i=>`<option ${i===x?'selected':''}>${esc(i)}</option>`).join('');$('#view').innerHTML=`<div class="card"><div class="fb" style="align-items:flex-end"><label style="flex:2">Buka<input id="q" placeholder="🔍  Naran, nú. rejistu, SSK…" oninput="mrows()"></label><button class="g" style="flex:0;white-space:nowrap;margin-bottom:1px" onclick="advToggle()">⚙ Buka avansadu <span id="vn" class="bd w"></span></button></div><div id="adv" class="fb" ${ADVOPEN?'':'hidden'} style="background:var(--soft);border:1px dashed var(--line);border-radius:12px;padding:12px;align-items:flex-end"><label>Data rejistu husi<input type="date" id="vf" value="${ADV.from}" onchange="advSet()"></label><label>Data rejistu to'o<input type="date" id="vt" value="${ADV.to}" onchange="advSet()"></label><label>Postu administrativu${isFP()&&U.ssk?`<select id="vp" disabled><option value="">🔒 ${esc(U.post)}</option></select>`:`<select id="vp" ${lock?'disabled':''} onchange="advPost()"><option value="">Postu hotu</option>${o(lock?[U.post]:postsOf(U.mun),ADV.post)}</select>`}</label><label>Hospital/SSK/Klinika Privada<select id="vs" onchange="advSet()"></select></label><label>Estadu<select id="vc" onchange="advSet()"><option value="">Hotu</option><option value="c" ${ADV.status==='c'?'selected':''}>Kompletu</option><option value="i" ${ADV.status==='i'?'selected':''}>Informasaun prinsipál falta</option></select></label><button class="g s" style="flex:0;margin-bottom:4px" onclick="advReset()">Reset</button></div><div class="tw"><table><tr><th>Nú. rejistu</th><th>Data rejistu</th><th>Naran kompletu</th><th>Hospital/SSK/Klinika Privada</th><th>Postu administrativu</th><th>Estadu</th><th>Asaun</th></tr><tbody id="mb"></tbody></table></div><p class="mut" id="mc"></p></div>`;advFac();mrows()}
function advToggle(){ADVOPEN=!ADVOPEN;$('#adv').hidden=!ADVOPEN}
function advFac(){$('#vs').innerHTML='<option value="">SSK hotu</option>'+sskList(ADV.post).map(x=>`<option ${x===ADV.ssk?'selected':''}>${esc(x)}</option>`).join('')}
function advPost(){ADV.post=$('#vp').value;ADV.ssk='';advFac();mrows()}
function advSet(){ADV.from=$('#vf').value;ADV.to=$('#vt').value;ADV.ssk=$('#vs').value;ADV.status=$('#vc').value;mrows()}
function advReset(){ADV={u:U.id,from:'',to:'',post:lockPost()?U.post:'',ssk:'',status:''};$('#vf').value=$('#vt').value=$('#vc').value='';if(!lockPost())$('#vp').value='';advFac();mrows()}
function mrows(){const q=$('#q').value.toLowerCase(),A=ADV,R=REC.filter(r=>(!q||(r.name+r.no+r.ssk+r.post).toLowerCase().includes(q))&&(!A.from||r.date>=A.from)&&(!A.to||r.date<=A.to)&&(!A.post||r.post===A.post)&&(!A.ssk||r.ssk===A.ssk)&&(!A.status||(A.status==='c'?!r.missing:r.missing>0))),n=[A.from||A.to,!isFP()&&A.post,A.ssk,A.status].filter(Boolean).length;$('#vn').textContent=n;$('#vn').hidden=!n;$('#mb').innerHTML=R.map(r=>`<tr><td><b>${esc(r.no)}</b></td><td>${esc(r.date)}</td><td>${esc(r.name)}</td><td>${esc(r.ssk)}</td><td>${esc(r.post)}</td><td>${r.missing?`<span class="bd w">Informasaun prinsipál falta ${r.missing}</span>`:'<span class="bd y">Kompletu</span>'}</td><td style="white-space:nowrap"><button class="s" onclick="edit('${esc(r.no)}')">Edita</button> <button class="s d" onclick="del('${esc(r.no)}')">Hamoos</button></td></tr>`).join('')||'<tr><td colspan=7 class="mut">Laiha rejistu</td></tr>';$('#mc').textContent='Rejistu '+R.length+' husi '+REC.length}
async function edit(no){const r=await api('getRecord',no);if(!r.ok){toast(r.error,1);return}EDIT=no;EDITREG=r.vals[RD];go('input',1);renderForm(r.vals)}
async function del(no){if(!await ask('Hamoos rejistu #'+no+'?'))return;const r=await api('deleteRecord',no);if(r.ok){toast('Rejistu hamoos ona.');DATA=null;manage()}else toast(r.error,1)}
const LVLO=[['Post administrative','Focal Point'],['Municipality','Administradór'],['Host','Host']];
async function accts(){if(!isHost()){go('overview');return}const L=await api('listAccounts'),MS=[...new Set(GEO.map(g=>g.m).filter(m=>!/^fora/i.test(m)))],o=(l,x)=>l.map(i=>`<option ${i===x?'selected':''}>${esc(i)}</option>`).join('');
const bdg=l=>`<span class="bd ${l==='Host'?'n':l==='Municipality'?'w':'y'}">${esc(tt(l))}</span>`;
$('#view').innerHTML=`<details class="sec" open><summary>➕ Aumenta konta</summary><div class="fg"><label class="fld"><span>ID</span><input id="ai" autocomplete="off"></label><label class="fld"><span>Email</span><input id="ae" autocomplete="off"></label><label class="fld"><span>Liafuan-pase</span><input id="ap" type="password" autocomplete="new-password"></label>
<label class="fld"><span>Nivel uzuáriu</span><select id="al" onchange="aSync()">${LVLO.map(([v,t])=>`<option value="${v}">${t}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>
<label class="fld"><span>Munisípiu</span><select id="am" onchange="aSync()">${o(MS,U.mun)}</select></label>
<label class="fld" id="aow"><span>Postu administrativu</span><select id="ao" onchange="aSsk()"></select></label>
<label class="fld" id="asw"><span>Hospital/SSK/Klinika Privada</span><select id="as"></select></label>
<label class="fld"><span>Aprovasaun</span><select id="aa"><option value="Yes">Sim</option><option value="No">Lae</option></select></label></div>
<p class="mut" style="margin:0 15px 10px;font-size:12px">Asaun: ✓ aprova · ✕ hasai aprovasaun · 🔑 troka liafuan-pase · 🏥 troka fasilidade (Focal Point) · 🗑 hamoos<br>👑 <b>Host</b>: asesu hotu no jere konta · 🛡️ <b>Administradór</b>: dadus hotu iha munisípiu, la iha Jestaun konta · 👤 <b>Focal Point</b>: de'it dadus husi nia Hospital/SSK/Klinika Privada (Munisípiu, Postu no SSK xave)</p>
<div style="padding:0 15px 14px"><button onclick="addAcc()">Kria konta</button></div></details>
<div class="card"><div class="tw"><table class="acc"><tr><th>ID</th><th>Email</th><th>Nivel</th><th>Munisípiu</th><th>Postu administrativu</th><th>Hospital/SSK/Klinika Privada</th><th>Aprovasaun</th><th>Asaun</th></tr>${L.map(a=>`<tr><td><b>${esc(a.id)}</b></td><td>${esc(a.email)}</td><td>${bdg(a.level)}</td><td>${esc(a.mun)}</td><td>${esc(a.level==='Post administrative'?a.post:'—')}</td><td>${a.level==='Post administrative'?(a.ssk?esc(a.ssk):'<span class="bd w">(postu tomak)</span>'):'—'}</td><td><span class="bd ${a.approval==='Yes'?'y':'n'}">${esc(tt(a.approval||'No'))}</span></td><td style="white-space:nowrap">${a.id===U.id?`<span class="bd y">Ita</span> <button class="s g" title="Troka liafuan-pase" onclick="pwAsk('${esc(a.id)}')">🔑</button>`:`<button class="s" title="Aprova konta" onclick="appr('${esc(a.id)}','Yes')">✓</button> <button class="s g" title="Hasai aprovasaun" onclick="appr('${esc(a.id)}','No')">✕</button> <button class="s g" title="Troka liafuan-pase" onclick="pwAsk('${esc(a.id)}')">🔑</button> ${a.level==='Post administrative'?`<button class="s g" title="Troka fasilidade (Postu / SSK)" onclick="areaAsk('${esc(a.id)}','${esc(a.mun)}','${esc(a.post)}','${esc(a.ssk||'')}')">🏥</button> `:''}<button class="s d" title="Hamoos konta" onclick="delAcc('${esc(a.id)}')">🗑</button>`}</td></tr>`).join('')}</table></div></div>`;aSync()}
function aSync(){const fp=$('#al').value==='Post administrative';$('#aow').hidden=!fp;$('#asw').hidden=!fp;$('#ao').innerHTML=postsOf($('#am').value).map(x=>`<option>${esc(x)}</option>`).join('');aSsk()}
function aSsk(){$('#as').innerHTML=ssksOf($('#am').value,$('#ao').value).map(x=>`<option>${esc(x)}</option>`).join('')}
async function appr(id,v){const r=await api('setApproval',id,v);r.ok?accts():toast(r.error,1)}
async function delAcc(id){if(!await ask('Hamoos konta "'+id+'"?'))return;const r=await api('deleteAccount',id);r.ok?accts():toast(r.error,1)}
async function addAcc(){const fp=$('#al').value==='Post administrative';const r=await api('addAccount',{id:$('#ai').value,email:$('#ae').value,password:$('#ap').value,level:$('#al').value,mun:$('#am').value,post:fp?$('#ao').value:'',ssk:fp?$('#as').value:'',approval:$('#aa').value});r.ok?(toast('Konta kria ona.'),accts()):toast(r.error,1)}
/* ===== Troka fasilidade konta Focal Point (Host) ===== */
function areaAsk(id,mun,post,ssk){const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='md';const P=postsOf(mun);
d.innerHTML=`<div style="max-width:360px"><p>🏥 Fasilidade ba <b>${esc(id)}</b></p><label class="fld"><span>Postu administrativu</span><select id="xp">${P.map(x=>`<option ${x===post?'selected':''}>${esc(x)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label class="fld"><span>Hospital/SSK/Klinika Privada</span><select id="xs"></select></label><small class="mut" style="display:block;margin:4px 0 12px">Focal Point haree de'it dadus husi fasilidade ne'e.</small><button class="g">Kansela</button> <button>Rai</button></div>`;
const xp=d.querySelector('#xp'),xs=d.querySelector('#xs'),fs=()=>{xs.innerHTML=ssksOf(mun,xp.value).map(x=>`<option ${x===ssk?'selected':''}>${esc(x)}</option>`).join('')};xp.onchange=fs;fs();
const b=d.querySelectorAll('button');b[0].onclick=()=>d.remove();b[1].onclick=async()=>{const r=await api('setAccountArea',id,xp.value,xs.value);if(r.ok){d.remove();toast('Fasilidade troka ona.');accts()}else toast(r.error,1)};document.body.append(d)}
/* ===== Troka liafuan-pase ===== */
function pwAsk(id){const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='md';d.innerHTML=`<div style="max-width:340px"><p>🔑 Liafuan-pase foun ba <b>${esc(id)}</b></p><input type="password" id="np1" placeholder="Liafuan-pase foun" autocomplete="new-password" style="margin-bottom:8px"><input type="password" id="np2" placeholder="Repete liafuan-pase" autocomplete="new-password"><small class="mut" style="display:block;margin:6px 0 12px">Karakter 8 ka liu, ho letra, númeru no karakter espesiál.</small><button class="g">Kansela</button> <button>Rai</button></div>`;
const b=d.querySelectorAll('button');b[0].onclick=()=>d.remove();b[1].onclick=async()=>{const a=d.querySelector('#np1').value,c=d.querySelector('#np2').value;if(a!==c){toast('Liafuan-pase la hanesan.',1);return}const r=await api('setPassword',id,a);if(r.ok){d.remove();toast('Liafuan-pase troka ona.')}else toast(r.error,1)};document.body.append(d);d.querySelector('#np1').focus()}
async function pwView(){const me=`<div class="card" style="max-width:520px;margin:0 auto 12px"><h4>🔒 Troka liafuan-pase</h4><p class="mut" style="text-align:center">Konta: <b>${esc(U.id)}</b> · ${esc(tt(U.level))} · ${esc(areaLbl())}</p><div style="text-align:center"><button onclick="pwAsk(U.id)">🔑 Troka ha'u-nia liafuan-pase</button></div></div>`;
$('#view').innerHTML=me;if(U.level!=='Municipality'&&!isHost())return;let L=[];try{L=await api('listPwAccounts')}catch(e){return}if(CUR!=='pw')return;
const lv=l=>l==='Host'?'Host':l==='Municipality'?'Administradór':'Focal Point';
$('#view').innerHTML=me+`<div class="card"><h4>🔑 Troka liafuan-pase ba uzuáriu seluk</h4><div class="fb" style="margin:0 0 8px"><label style="flex:1">Buka<input id="pwq" placeholder="🔍 ID, nivel ka SSK…" oninput="pwFil()"></label></div><div class="tw"><table class="acc"><tr><th>ID</th><th>Nivel</th><th>Postu administrativu</th><th>Hospital/SSK/Klinika Privada</th><th>Asaun</th></tr><tbody id="pwb">${L.filter(a=>a.id!==U.id).map(a=>`<tr data-q="${esc((a.id+' '+lv(a.level)+' '+(a.post||'')+' '+(a.ssk||'')).toLowerCase())}"><td><b>${esc(a.id)}</b></td><td>${esc(lv(a.level))}</td><td>${esc(a.post||'—')}</td><td>${esc(a.ssk||'—')}</td><td><button class="s" onclick="pwAsk('${esc(a.id)}')">🔑 Troka</button></td></tr>`).join('')||'<tr><td colspan="5" class="mut">Laiha konta</td></tr>'}</tbody></table></div></div>`}
function pwFil(){const q=$('#pwq').value.toLowerCase();document.querySelectorAll('#pwb tr').forEach(r=>r.hidden=!!q&&!(r.dataset.q||'').includes(q))}
/* Bandeira Timor-Leste ne'ebé lalin iha pájina login */
(()=>{const a=document.getElementById('auth');if(!a||a.querySelector('.tlwrap'))return;const w=document.createElement('div');w.className='tlwrap';w.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
const N=36;let h='<span class="tlpole"></span><div class="tlflag">';for(let i=0;i<N;i++){const amp=(1.5+i*0.32).toFixed(2);h+=`<i style="--tlw:${N*100}%;background-position:${(i/(N-1)*100).toFixed(3)}% 0;--a:${amp}px;animation-delay:${(-i*0.075).toFixed(3)}s"></i>`}
w.innerHTML=h+'</div>';a.prepend(w)})();
